        function webUniqueCount(raw){
            raw=String(raw||"");if(!raw)return 0;
            /* Qidian mobile catalog may embed chapter URLs inside JSON:
               \/chapter\/... or \u002Fchapter\u002F... .
               This preselector only counts IDs; final correctness still belongs to WEB-EXACT/SET-EXACT. */
            var norm=raw
                .replace(/\\u002[fF]/g,"/")
                .replace(/\\\//g,"/")
                .replace(/&#x2[fF];/gi,"/")
                .replace(/&#47;/gi,"/"),
                safeBid=String(bid||"").replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,"\\$&"),
                re=new RegExp("/chapter/"+safeBid+"/(\\d+)","ig"),
                seen={},n=0,m;
            while((m=re.exec(norm))){
                var id=String(m[1]||"");
                if(id&&!seen[id]){seen[id]=1;n++;}
                if(n>60000)break;
            }
            return n;
        }
        function valid(x){return !!(x&&x.stats&&Number(x.stats.chapters||0)>0);}
        function better(a,b){
            if(!valid(a))return b;if(!valid(b))return a;
            var ac=Number(a.stats.chapters||0),bc=Number(b.stats.chapters||0),
                at=Number(a.stats.time||0),bt=Number(b.stats.time||0);
            if(at!==bt)return at>bt?a:b;
            if(ac!==bc)return ac>bc?a:b;
            return String(b.tag||"").indexOf("simple1")>=0?b:a;
        }
        var wc=webUniqueCount(webRaw),pick=null,reason="";
        if(wc>0){
            var e0c=valid(m0)&&Number(m0.stats.chapters||0)===wc,
                e1c=valid(m1)&&Number(m1.stats.chapters||0)===wc;
            if(e0c&&e1c){pick=better(m0,m1);reason="web-count-both";}
            else if(e0c){pick=m0;reason="web-count-simple0";}
            else if(e1c){pick=m1;reason="web-count-simple1";}
        }
        if(!pick){pick=better(m0,m1);reason=reason||"coverage-fallback";}
        if(pick){
            var all=[];
            if(valid(m0))all.push("s0="+m0.stats.chapters+"/"+m0.stats.time);
            if(valid(m1))all.push("s1="+m1.stats.chapters+"/"+m1.stats.time);
            pick.detail="dual-parallel "+reason+" webUnique="+wc+" extractor="+(wc>0?"escaped-url-ok":"no-id")+" "+all.join(" ")+" | "+String(pick.detail||"");
        }
        return {ok:valid(pick),reason:reason||"simple-empty",simple:pick,simple0:m0,simple1:m1,webRaw:webRaw,webUnique:wc,ms:elapsed,parallel:true};
    }
return {app:app,v3:v3,simple:simple,pager:pager,webCategory:webCategory,simpleFromRaw:simpleFromRaw,parallelCold:parallelCold};

var QF_CatalogMerger=(function(){
    function normTitle(v){
        try{return qfNormChapter(v);}catch(_e){return String(v||"").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,"");}
    }
    function merge(base,donors){
        base=base&&Array.isArray(base.chapters)?base:{chapters:[],volumes:[],source:""};donors=Array.isArray(donors)?donors:[];
        var idMap={},titleMap={},matched=0,timeFill=0,wordFill=0,titleMatch=0;
        for(var di=0;di<donors.length;di++){
            var d=donors[di]&&Array.isArray(donors[di].chapters)?donors[di].chapters:[];
            for(var i=0;i<d.length;i++){
                var x=d[i]||{};if(!x.id)continue;
                var old=idMap[x.id];
                if(!old||(!String(old.time||"")&&String(x.time||""))||(!String(old.words||"")&&String(x.words||"")))idMap[x.id]=x;
                var nt=normTitle(x.name);if(nt&&!titleMap[nt])titleMap[nt]=x;
            }
        }
        var c=base.chapters;
        for(var j=0;j<c.length;j++){
            var b=c[j]||{},m=idMap[b.id],byTitle=false;
            if(!m){var ntb=normTitle(b.name);if(ntb&&titleMap[ntb]){m=titleMap[ntb];byTitle=true;}}
            if(!m)continue;matched++;if(byTitle)titleMatch++;
            if((b.time===undefined||b.time===null||String(b.time).trim()==="")&&m.time!==undefined&&m.time!==null&&String(m.time).trim()!==""){b.time=m.time;timeFill++;}
            if((b.words===undefined||b.words===null||String(b.words).trim()==="")&&m.words!==undefined&&m.words!==null&&String(m.words).trim()!==""){b.words=m.words;wordFill++;}
            if(!b.volumeName&&m.volumeName)b.volumeName=m.volumeName;if(!b.volumeId&&m.volumeId)b.volumeId=m.volumeId;
        }
        for(var k=0;k<c.length;k++)c[k].index=k;
        return {model:base,matched:matched,timeFill:timeFill,wordFill:wordFill,titleMatch:titleMatch};
    }
        function mergeAligned(base,donor){
        if(!base||!Array.isArray(base.chapters)||!donor||!Array.isArray(donor.chapters))return null;
        var c=base.chapters,d=donor.chapters;if(!c.length||c.length!==d.length)return null;
        var matched=0,timeFill=0,wordFill=0,chapters=0,time=0,words=0;
        for(var i=0;i<c.length;i++){
            var b=c[i]||{},x=d[i]||{};
            if(!b.id||!x.id||String(b.id)!==String(x.id))return null;
            if(!b.name)return null;
            matched++;
            if((b.time===undefined||b.time===null||String(b.time).trim()==="")&&x.time!==undefined&&x.time!==null&&String(x.time).trim()!==""){b.time=x.time;timeFill++;}
            if((b.words===undefined||b.words===null||String(b.words).trim()==="")&&x.words!==undefined&&x.words!==null&&String(x.words).trim()!==""){b.words=x.words;wordFill++;}
            if(!b.volumeName&&x.volumeName)b.volumeName=x.volumeName;
            if(!b.volumeId&&x.volumeId)b.volumeId=x.volumeId;
            b.index=i;chapters++;
            if(b.time!==undefined&&b.time!==null&&String(b.time).trim()!=="")time++;
            if(b.words!==undefined&&b.words!==null&&String(b.words).trim()!=="")words++;
        }
        return {model:base,matched:matched,timeFill:timeFill,wordFill:wordFill,titleMatch:0,stats:{chapters:chapters,time:time,words:words},fast:"ALIGNED-ID"};
    }
return {merge:merge,mergeAligned:mergeAligned};
})();

var QF_LegadoCatalogAdapter=(function(){
    function fmtTime(v){
        if(v===undefined||v===null||v==="")return "";
        var raw=String(v).trim(),n=Number(raw),d=null;
        if(isFinite(n)&&n>946684800){if(n<1e12)n*=1000;d=new Date(n);}
        if(!d){
            var em=raw.match(/(?:^|\D)(1\d{9}|1\d{12})(?:\D|$)/);if(em){var en=Number(em[1]);if(en<1e12)en*=1000;d=new Date(en);}
        }
        if(!d){
            var clean=raw.replace(/年/g,"-").replace(/月/g,"-").replace(/日/g," ").replace(/[T]/g," ").replace(/\.(?=\d{1,2}(?:\D|$))/g,"-").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();
            var p=Date.parse(clean.replace(/-/g,"/"));if(!isNaN(p))d=new Date(p);
        }
        if(!d||isNaN(d.getTime()))return raw;
        function z(x){return x<10?"0"+x:String(x);}return d.getFullYear()+"-"+z(d.getMonth()+1)+"-"+z(d.getDate())+" "+z(d.getHours())+":"+z(d.getMinutes());
    }
    function fmtWords(v){
        if(v===undefined||v===null||v==="")return "";var s=String(v).replace(/,/g,"").trim(),m=s.match(/([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)(万|千|百)?\s*字?/i);if(!m)return "";var n=Number(m[1]);if(!isFinite(n)||n<=0)return "";return (Math.floor(n)===n?String(Math.floor(n)):String(n))+String(m[2]||"")+"字";
    }
    function info(tm,wc){var t=fmtTime(tm),w=fmtWords(wc);return t&&w?t+"  "+w:(t||w||"");}
    function chapterUrl(bookId,c,bookName,author,src){
        var u="https://www.qidian.com/chapter/"+encodeURIComponent(String(bookId||""))+"/"+encodeURIComponent(String(c.id||""))+"/";
        u+="?qfVip="+(c.vip?"1":"0")+"&qfTitle="+encodeURIComponent(String(c.name||""))+"&qfBook="+encodeURIComponent(String(bookName||""))+"&qfAuthor="+encodeURIComponent(String(author||""))+"&qfTocSrc="+encodeURIComponent(String(src||"cleanroom"))+"&qfIndex="+Math.max(0,Number(c.index||0));
        if(/地图|插图|彩蛋|设定图|人物图|示意图|平面图|世界图|区域图/i.test(String(c.name||"")))u+="&qfMediaRev=67";return u;
    }
    function copyrightRow(bookId,bookName,author){
        return {N:"版权信息",C:"https://m.qidian.com/book/"+encodeURIComponent(String(bookId||""))+"/?qfType=copyright&qfBook="+encodeURIComponent(String(bookName||""))+"&qfAuthor="+encodeURIComponent(String(author||""))+"&qfCv=3101",V:false,Vo:false,T:""};
    }
    function rows(model,meta){
        model=model||{chapters:[],volumes:[]};meta=meta||{};var out=[copyrightRow(meta.bookId,meta.bookName,meta.author)],cs=Array.isArray(model.chapters)?model.chapters:[];
        var lastVol=null;
        for(var i=0;i<cs.length;i++){
            var c=cs[i]||{};if(!c.id||!c.name)continue;
            var vn=String(c.volumeName||"").trim();
            if(vn&&vn!==lastVol){out.push({N:vn,C:"",V:false,Vo:true,T:""});lastVol=vn;}
            c.index=i;out.push({N:String(c.name),C:chapterUrl(meta.bookId,c,meta.bookName,meta.author,meta.source||model.source),V:!!c.vip,Vo:false,T:info(c.time,c.words)});
        }
        return out;
    }
    function stats(rows){var z={chapters:0,time:0,words:0},a=Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[];for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){var x=a[i]||{};if(x.Vo||x.N==="版权信息"||!x.C)continue;z.chapters++;var t=String(x.T||"");if(/20\d{2}[-\/.年]/.test(t)||/\d{10,13}/.test(t))z.time++;if(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:万|千|百)?字/.test(t))z.words++;}return z;}
        function rowsWithStats(model,meta){
        model=model||{chapters:[],volumes:[]};meta=meta||{};
        var out=[copyrightRow(meta.bookId,meta.bookName,meta.author)],cs=Array.isArray(model.chapters)?model.chapters:[],
            lastVol=null,z={chapters:0,time:0,words:0};
        for(var i=0;i<cs.length;i++){
            var c=cs[i]||{};if(!c.id||!c.name)continue;
            var vn=String(c.volumeName||"").trim();
            if(vn&&vn!==lastVol){out.push({N:vn,C:"",V:false,Vo:true,T:""});lastVol=vn;}
            c.index=i;
            var inf=info(c.time,c.words);
            out.push({N:String(c.name),C:chapterUrl(meta.bookId,c,meta.bookName,meta.author,meta.source||model.source),V:!!c.vip,Vo:false,T:inf});
            z.chapters++;
            if(/20\d{2}[-\/.年]/.test(inf)||/\d{10,13}/.test(inf))z.time++;
            if(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:万|千|百)?字/.test(inf))z.words++;
        }
        try{out._qfStats=z;}catch(_s){}
        return {rows:out,stats:z};
    }
return {rows:rows,rowsWithStats:rowsWithStats,stats:stats,fmtTime:fmtTime,fmtWords:fmtWords,info:info,chapterUrl:chapterUrl};
})();

var QF_CatalogCompleteness=(function(){
    function nonEmpty(v){return v!==undefined&&v!==null&&String(v).trim()!=="";}
    function maps(donors){
        var dm={},tm={},a=Array.isArray(donors)?donors:[];
        for(var di=0;di<a.length;di++){
            var cs=a[di]&&Array.isArray(a[di].chapters)?a[di].chapters:[];
            for(var i=0;i<cs.length;i++){
                var x=cs[i]||{},id=String(x.id||"");if(id){var o=dm[id]||{};if(!nonEmpty(o.time)&&nonEmpty(x.time))o.time=x.time;if(!nonEmpty(o.words)&&nonEmpty(x.words))o.words=x.words;dm[id]=o;}
                var nt="";try{nt=qfNormChapter(x.name);}catch(_e){nt=String(x.name||"").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,"");}
                if(nt){var t=tm[nt]||{};if(!nonEmpty(t.time)&&nonEmpty(x.time))t.time=x.time;if(!nonEmpty(t.words)&&nonEmpty(x.words))t.words=x.words;tm[nt]=t;}
            }
        }
        return {id:dm,title:tm};
    }
    function valueFor(b,field,m){
        if(nonEmpty(b&&b[field]))return b[field];var id=String(b&&b.id||""),x=id&&m.id[id]?m.id[id]:null;if(x&&nonEmpty(x[field]))return x[field];
        var nt="";try{nt=qfNormChapter(b&&b.name);}catch(_e){nt=String(b&&b.name||"").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,"");}var t=nt&&m.title[nt]?m.title[nt]:null;return t&&nonEmpty(t[field])?t[field]:"";
    }
    function coverage(base,donors){
        var cs=base&&Array.isArray(base.chapters)?base.chapters:[],m=maps(donors),z={chapters:0,time:0,words:0};
        for(var i=0;i<cs.length;i++){var b=cs[i]||{};if(!b.id||!b.name)continue;z.chapters++;if(nonEmpty(valueFor(b,"time",m)))z.time++;if(nonEmpty(valueFor(b,"words",m)))z.words++;}return z;
    }
    function missing(base,donors,field,max){
        var cs=base&&Array.isArray(base.chapters)?base.chapters:[],m=maps(donors),out={chapters:[],volumes:[],source:"CATALOG-MISSING-"+String(field||"")},lim=Number(max||0);
        for(var i=0;i<cs.length;i++){var b=cs[i]||{};if(!b.id||!b.name)continue;if(!nonEmpty(valueFor(b,field,m))){out.chapters.push(b);if(lim>0&&out.chapters.length>=lim)break;}}return out;
    }
    return {coverage:coverage,missing:missing};
})();

var QF_CatalogService=(function(){
    var CACHE_SCHEMA=6,FRESH_MS=30*60*1000,WARM_MS=6*60*60*1000,DELTA_MAX=12;
    function bookMeta(ctx,baseUrl){
        var bid="",name="",author="",latest="",kind="";
        try{bid=String(qfBookVarV09.call(ctx,"qf_bid","")||"");name=String(qfBookVarV09.call(ctx,"qf_name","")||"");author=String(qfBookVarV09.call(ctx,"qf_author","")||"");latest=String(qfBookVarV09.call(ctx,"qf_lastChapter_v70","")||"");kind=String(qfBookVarV09.call(ctx,"qf_kind","")||"");}catch(_e){}
        if(!bid){var m=String(baseUrl||"").match(/(?:book\/|bookId=)(\d+)/i);if(m)bid=String(m[1]);}
        return {bookId:bid,bookName:name,author:author,latest:latest,kind:kind};
    }
    function perf(report,path,start,target){report.performance={path:String(path||""),elapsed:Math.max(0,Date.now()-Number(start||Date.now())),target:String(target||"")};}
    function chooseSkeleton(models){
        var best=null,bestScore=-1;models=Array.isArray(models)?models:[];
        for(var i=0;i<models.length;i++){
            var mm=models[i];if(!mm||!Array.isArray(mm.chapters))continue;
            var s=QF_CatalogNormalizer.stats(mm),score=s.chapters*100000+s.time*10+s.words;
            if(score>bestScore){best=mm;bestScore=score;}
        }
        return best;
    }
    function missingTimeModel(base,donor){
        var out={chapters:[],volumes:[],source:"APP-pager-time-focus"},have={},dc=donor&&Array.isArray(donor.chapters)?donor.chapters:[];
        for(var i=0;i<dc.length;i++){var d=dc[i]||{};if(d.id&&d.time!==undefined&&d.time!==null&&String(d.time).trim()!=="")have[String(d.id)]=1;}
        var bc=base&&Array.isArray(base.chapters)?base.chapters:[];
        for(var j=0;j<bc.length;j++){var b=bc[j]||{};if(b.id&&!have[String(b.id)]&&(b.time===undefined||b.time===null||String(b.time).trim()===""))out.chapters.push(b);}
        return out;
    }
    function chapterRows(rows){
        var out=[],a=Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[];
        for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){var x=a[i]||{};if(x.Vo||x.N==="版权信息"||!x.C)continue;out.push(x);}
        return out;
    }
    function chapterIdFromRow(x){
        var u=String(x&&x.C||""),m=u.match(/\/chapter\/\d+\/(\d+)/i)||u.match(/[?&#](?:chapterId|cid)=(\d+)/i);return m&&m[1]?String(m[1]):"";
    }
    function vipFromRow(x){
        if(x&&typeof x.V==="boolean")return x.V;
        var m=String(x&&x.C||"").match(/[?&#]qfVip=([01])/i);return m?m[1]==="1":null;
    }
    function lastVolumeName(rows){
        var a=Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[],v="";
        for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){var x=a[i]||{};if(x.Vo&&x.N)v=String(x.N||"").trim();}
        return v;
    }
    function lastChapterIdFromRows(rows){
        var c=chapterRows(rows);return c.length?chapterIdFromRow(c[c.length-1]):"";
    }
    function prefixTailMatches(rows,simpleChapters,count){
        var cr=chapterRows(rows),sc=Array.isArray(simpleChapters)?simpleChapters:[],n=Math.min(5,Number(count||0));
        if(!n||cr.length<count||sc.length<count)return false;
        for(var i=count-n;i<count;i++){if(chapterIdFromRow(cr[i])!==String(sc[i]&&sc[i].id||""))return false;}
        return true;
    }
    function stableTailVip(rows){
        var cr=chapterRows(rows),n=Math.min(5,cr.length);if(n<3)return {ok:false,vip:false};
        var first=vipFromRow(cr[cr.length-n]);if(first===null)return {ok:false,vip:false};
        for(var i=cr.length-n+1;i<cr.length;i++){if(vipFromRow(cr[i])!==first)return {ok:false,vip:false};}
        return {ok:true,vip:!!first};
    }
    function canDelta(cached,simpleModel,cc,pc){
        var sc=simpleModel&&Array.isArray(simpleModel.chapters)?simpleModel.chapters:[],delta=pc-cc;
        if(delta<=0||delta>DELTA_MAX)return {ok:false,reason:"delta-size",delta:delta};
        if(!prefixTailMatches(cached.rows,sc,cc))return {ok:false,reason:"prefix-mismatch",delta:delta};
        var sv=stableTailVip(cached.rows);if(!sv.ok)return {ok:false,reason:"vip-unstable",delta:delta};
        var lv=lastVolumeName(cached.rows),firstNew=sc[cc]||{},nv=String(firstNew.volumeName||"").trim();
        if(lv&&nv&&lv!==nv)return {ok:false,reason:"volume-change",delta:delta};
        for(var i=cc;i<pc;i++){var x=sc[i]||{};if(!x.id||!x.name)return {ok:false,reason:"incomplete-simple",delta:delta};var xv=String(x.volumeName||"").trim();if(lv&&xv&&xv!==lv)return {ok:false,reason:"volume-change",delta:delta};}
        return {ok:true,reason:"safe-tail",delta:delta,vip:sv.vip,volume:lv};
    }
    function appendDelta(cached,simpleModel,meta,check){
        var rows=Array.isArray(cached.rows)?cached.rows.slice():[],sc=simpleModel&&Array.isArray(simpleModel.chapters)?simpleModel.chapters:[],cc=Number((cached.stats||{}).chapters||chapterRows(rows).length||0);
        var currentVol=lastVolumeName(rows),added=0;
        for(var i=cc;i<sc.length;i++){
            var c=sc[i]||{},vn=String(c.volumeName||"").trim();
            if(vn&&vn!==currentVol){rows.push({N:vn,C:"",V:false,Vo:true,T:""});currentVol=vn;}
            c.vip=!!check.vip;c.index=i;
            rows.push({N:String(c.name||""),C:QF_LegadoCatalogAdapter.chapterUrl(meta.bookId,c,meta.bookName,meta.author,"CACHE-DELTA"),V:!!c.vip,Vo:false,T:QF_LegadoCatalogAdapter.info(c.time,c.words)});
            added++;
        }
        return {rows:rows,added:added,stats:QF_LegadoCatalogAdapter.stats(rows)};
    }
    function cacheKey(bid){return QF_CacheManager.key("catalog41","qidian",bid);}
    function legacyCacheSkeleton(ctx,bid,report,donors){
        try{
            var oldKey=qfCacheKeyV56("toc76","起点",bid),old=qfCacheGet.call(ctx,oldKey);
            if(old&&Array.isArray(old.list)){
                var model=QF_CatalogNormalizer.fromLegacyRows(old.list,"LEGACY-CACHE76"),s=QF_CatalogNormalizer.stats(model);
                if(s.chapters>0){
                    QF_CatalogReport.stage(report,"RECOVERY-legacy-cache76",{ok:true,chapters:s.chapters,time:s.time,words:s.words,detail:"cache"});
                    if(Array.isArray(donors))donors.push(model);
                    return model;
                }
            }
        }catch(e){QF_CatalogReport.error(report,"recovery-legacy-cache76",e);}
        return null;
    }
    function cacheReturn(ctx,key,cached,report,source,now,start,path,target){
        report.cache=true;
        report.output=cached.stats||QF_LegadoCatalogAdapter.stats(cached.rows);
        report.output.source=String(source||cached.source||"CACHE41");
        report.budget={wordRequests:0,fullRefresh:"dual-simple+webid-fix+exact-set+warm-revalidate+cpu-fast",warmProbe:"getsimple-only",deltaMax:DELTA_MAX};
        perf(report,path||"cache",start,target||"fresh≤50ms");
        QF_CatalogReport.save(ctx,report);
        if(now&&cached){cached.ts=now;try{QF_CacheManager.set(ctx,key,cached);}catch(_e){}}
        return cached.rows;
    }
    function legacySkeleton(ctx,raw,baseUrl,report,donors,simple,prefetch){
        try{
            var expectedIds=[],sc=simple&&simple.model&&Array.isArray(simple.model.chapters)?simple.model.chapters:[];

            prefetch=prefetch&&typeof prefetch==="object"?prefetch:{};
            var rows=qfTocLoadLegacyV70.call(ctx,raw,baseUrl,{skeletonOnly:expectedIds.length>0,expectedCount:expectedIds.length,expectedIds:expectedIds,prefetchWebRaw:String(prefetch.webRaw||""),parallelMs:Number(prefetch.ms||0)});
            var fast=String(rows&&rows._qfSkeletonFast||''),leanAudit=String(rows&&rows._qfSkeletonAudit||'');
            var model=QF_CatalogNormalizer.fromLegacyRows(rows,fast==="WEB-SET-EXACT"?"LEGACY-WEB-SET-EXACT":(fast==="WEB-EXACT"?"LEGACY-WEB-EXACT":(fast?"LEGACY-V3-EXACT":"LEGACY-SKELETON"))),s=QF_CatalogNormalizer.stats(model);
            QF_CatalogReport.stage(report,fast==="WEB-SET-EXACT"?"RECOVERY-web-set-exact":(fast==="WEB-EXACT"?"RECOVERY-web-exact":(fast?"RECOVERY-legacy-v3-exact":"RECOVERY-legacy-skeleton")),{ok:s.chapters>0,chapters:s.chapters,time:s.time,words:s.words,detail:s.chapters?((fast?("fast="+fast+" / skipped="+((fast==="WEB-EXACT"||fast==="WEB-SET-EXACT")?"v3+category":"duplicate-enrichment")+(fast==="WEB-SET-EXACT"?" / reordered-by-simple":"")):"canonicalized")+(leanAudit?(" | LeanAudit "+leanAudit):"")):"empty"});
            if(s.chapters>0){if(Array.isArray(donors))donors.push(model);return model;}
        }catch(e){QF_CatalogReport.error(report,"recovery-legacy-skeleton",e);}

    }

            var model=QF_CatalogNormalizer.fromLegacyRows(rows,fast==="WEB-SET-EXACT"?"LEGACY-WEB-SET-EXACT":(fast==="WEB-EXACT"?"LEGACY-WEB-EXACT":(fast?"LEGACY-V3-EXACT":"LEGACY-SKELETON"))),s=QF_CatalogNormalizer.stats(model);
            QF_CatalogReport.stage(report,fast==="WEB-SET-EXACT"?"RECOVERY-web-set-exact":(fast==="WEB-EXACT"?"RECOVERY-web-exact":(fast?"RECOVERY-legacy-v3-exact":"RECOVERY-legacy-skeleton")),{ok:s.chapters>0,chapters:s.chapters,time:s.time,words:s.words,detail:s.chapters?((fast?("fast="+fast+" / skipped="+((fast==="WEB-EXACT"||fast==="WEB-SET-EXACT")?"v3+category":"duplicate-enrichment")+(fast==="WEB-SET-EXACT"?" / reordered-by-simple":"")):"canonicalized")+(leanAudit?(" | LeanAudit "+leanAudit):"")):"empty"});
            if(s.chapters>0){if(Array.isArray(donors))donors.push(model);return model;}
        }catch(e){QF_CatalogReport.error(report,"recovery-legacy-skeleton",e);}
        return null;
    }
    function fallbackSkeletons(ctx,bid,raw,report,donors){
        var models=[];
        var w=QF_CatalogClient.webCategory(ctx,bid);
        QF_CatalogReport.stage(report,w.tag||"WEB-category",{ok:w.ok,chapters:w.stats.chapters,time:w.stats.time,words:w.stats.words,ms:w.ms,detail:w.detail});
        if(w.stats.chapters){models.push(w.model);donors.push(w.model);}
        var v3=QF_CatalogClient.v3(ctx,bid);
        QF_CatalogReport.stage(report,v3.tag||"APP-v3",{ok:v3.ok,chapters:v3.stats.chapters,time:v3.stats.time,words:v3.stats.words,ms:v3.ms,code:v3.code,detail:v3.detail});
        if(v3.stats.chapters){models.push(v3.model);donors.push(v3.model);}
        var hm=QF_CatalogNormalizer.fromHtml(raw,"HTML-current"),hs=QF_CatalogNormalizer.stats(hm);
        QF_CatalogReport.stage(report,"HTML-current",{ok:hs.chapters>0,chapters:hs.chapters,time:hs.time,words:hs.words,detail:hs.chapters?"":"empty"});
        if(hs.chapters){models.push(hm);donors.push(hm);}
        return chooseSkeleton(models);
    }
    function load(raw,baseUrl){
        var ctx=this,start=Date.now(),meta=bookMeta(ctx,baseUrl),bid=String(meta.bookId||"");if(!bid)return [];
        var report=QF_CatalogReport.empty(bid),key=cacheKey(bid),now=Date.now(),cached=QF_CacheManager.get(ctx,key,null),probeSimple=null;
        report.budget={wordRequests:0,fullRefresh:"dual-simple+webid-fix+exact-set+warm-revalidate+cpu-fast",warmProbe:"getsimple-only",deltaMax:DELTA_MAX};

        if(cached&&Number(cached.schema||0)===CACHE_SCHEMA&&Array.isArray(cached.rows)&&Number(cached.ts||0)>0){
            var age=now-Number(cached.ts||0),ageMin=Math.max(0,Math.round(age/60000));
            if(age<FRESH_MS){
                report.incremental={mode:"fresh-cache",ageMin:ageMin,cached:Number((cached.stats||{}).chapters||0),probe:0,same:true};
                return cacheReturn(ctx,key,cached,report,"CACHE-FRESH",0,start,"fresh-cache","≤50ms");
            }
            if(age<WARM_MS){
                var cc=Number((cached.stats||{}).chapters||QF_LegadoCatalogAdapter.stats(cached.rows).chapters||0),lastCached=lastChapterIdFromRows(cached.rows);
                probeSimple=QF_CatalogClient.simple(ctx,bid,cc);
                var pc=Number(probeSimple&&probeSimple.stats&&probeSimple.stats.chapters||0),
                    pcs=probeSimple&&probeSimple.model&&Array.isArray(probeSimple.model.chapters)?probeSimple.model.chapters:[],
                    lastProbe=pcs.length?String(pcs[pcs.length-1].id||""):"";
                var same=!!(probeSimple&&probeSimple.ok&&pc===cc&&lastCached&&lastProbe&&lastCached===lastProbe);
                report.incremental={mode:"warm-probe",ageMin:ageMin,cached:cc,probe:pc,same:same};
                QF_CatalogReport.stage(report,"INCREMENTAL-getsimple",{ok:!!(probeSimple&&probeSimple.ok),chapters:pc,time:Number(probeSimple&&probeSimple.stats&&probeSimple.stats.time||0),words:Number(probeSimple&&probeSimple.stats&&probeSimple.stats.words||0),ms:Number(probeSimple&&probeSimple.ms||0),code:String(probeSimple&&probeSimple.code||""),detail:same?"unchanged":"refresh"});
                if(same)return cacheReturn(ctx,key,cached,report,"CACHE-PROBE",now,start,"warm-probe","≤450ms");

                if(probeSimple&&probeSimple.ok&&pc>cc){
                    var chk=canDelta(cached,probeSimple.model,cc,pc);
                    report.incremental={mode:"warm-delta",ageMin:ageMin,cached:cc,probe:pc,same:false,delta:Number(chk.delta||0),reason:String(chk.reason||"")};
                    if(chk.ok){
                        var ap=appendDelta(cached,probeSimple.model,meta,chk);
                        if(ap.added===Number(chk.delta||0)&&ap.stats.chapters===pc){
                            var nc={schema:CACHE_SCHEMA,rows:ap.rows,stats:ap.stats,source:"CACHE-DELTA",ts:now,lastChapterId:lastChapterIdFromRows(ap.rows)};
                            report.output={chapters:ap.stats.chapters,time:ap.stats.time,words:ap.stats.words,source:"CACHE-DELTA"};
                            report.completeness={chapters:ap.stats.chapters,time:ap.stats.time,words:ap.stats.words,wordsOptional:true,wordDonor:"optional/unsupported"};
                            perf(report,"delta-append",start,"≤550ms");
                            QF_CatalogReport.save(ctx,report);QF_CacheManager.set(ctx,key,nc);
                            return ap.rows;
                        }
                        report.incremental.reason="delta-build-mismatch";
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        var donors=[],skeleton=null,phase={simple:0,skeleton:0,merge:0,adapter:0},coldPack=null,tSimple=Date.now(),
            probeChanged=!!(probeSimple&&report.incremental&&report.incremental.same===false);
        if(probeChanged||!(probeSimple&&probeSimple.stats&&probeSimple.stats.chapters)){
            try{coldPack=QF_CatalogClient.parallelCold(ctx,bid);}catch(_pc){coldPack=null;}
        }
        var simple=(!probeChanged&&probeSimple&&probeSimple.stats&&probeSimple.stats.chapters)?probeSimple:
            (coldPack&&coldPack.ok&&coldPack.simple?coldPack.simple:
            (probeSimple&&probeSimple.stats&&probeSimple.stats.chapters?probeSimple:QF_CatalogClient.simple(ctx,bid,0)));
        phase.simple=Math.max(0,Date.now()-tSimple);
        QF_CatalogReport.stage(report,simple.tag||"APP-simple",{ok:simple.ok,chapters:simple.stats.chapters,time:simple.stats.time,words:simple.stats.words,ms:simple.ms,code:simple.code,detail:(probeSimple===simple?"reuse-probe ":(coldPack&&coldPack.ok?(probeChanged?"warm-change-revalidate dual-parallel ":"parallel-simple0+1+web "):""))+String(simple.detail||"")});
        if(simple.stats.chapters)donors.push(simple.model);

        var tSkeleton=Date.now();
        if(simple.stats.chapters){
            skeleton=legacyCacheSkeleton(ctx,bid,report,donors);
            if(!skeleton)skeleton=legacySkeleton(ctx,raw,baseUrl,report,donors,simple,coldPack);
            if(!skeleton){
                skeleton=fallbackSkeletons(ctx,bid,raw,report,donors);
                if(!skeleton){skeleton=simple.model;skeleton.source="APP-simple-skeleton";QF_CatalogReport.stage(report,"RECOVERY-simple-skeleton",{ok:true,chapters:simple.stats.chapters,time:simple.stats.time,words:simple.stats.words,detail:"direct"});}
            }
        }else{
            skeleton=legacyCacheSkeleton(ctx,bid,report,donors);
            if(!skeleton)skeleton=fallbackSkeletons(ctx,bid,raw,report,donors);
            if(!skeleton)skeleton=legacySkeleton(ctx,raw,baseUrl,report,donors);
        }
        phase.skeleton=Math.max(0,Date.now()-tSkeleton);

        if(skeleton){
            var stBefore=QF_CatalogNormalizer.stats(skeleton),need=Math.max(3,Math.floor(stBefore.chapters*0.60));
            if(stBefore.time<need&&simple.stats.time<need){
                var pg=QF_CatalogClient.pager(ctx,bid,skeleton,{tag:"APP-pager-time",anchors:7});
                QF_CatalogReport.stage(report,pg.tag,{ok:pg.ok,chapters:pg.stats.chapters,time:pg.stats.time,words:pg.stats.words,ms:pg.ms,detail:pg.detail});
                if(pg.stats.chapters)donors.push(pg.model);
            }else if(simple.stats.chapters&&simple.stats.time<stBefore.chapters){
                var miss=missingTimeModel(skeleton,simple.model),missCount=miss.chapters.length;
                if(missCount>0&&missCount<=20){
                    var pg2=QF_CatalogClient.pager(ctx,bid,miss,{tag:"APP-pager-time-focus",anchors:Math.min(7,missCount)});
                    QF_CatalogReport.stage(report,pg2.tag,{ok:pg2.ok,chapters:pg2.stats.chapters,time:pg2.stats.time,words:pg2.stats.words,ms:pg2.ms,detail:"missing="+missCount+" "+String(pg2.detail||"")});
                    if(pg2.stats.chapters)donors.push(pg2.model);
                }
            }
        }

        if(!skeleton){
            report.errors.push("cleanroom skeleton empty");
            perf(report,"full-failed",start,"≤1500ms");QF_CatalogReport.save(ctx,report);
            try{
                var legacy=qfTocLoadLegacyV70.call(ctx,raw,baseUrl);
                if(Array.isArray(legacy)&&legacy.length){
                    var ls=QF_LegadoCatalogAdapter.stats(legacy);
                    report.output={chapters:ls.chapters,time:ls.time,words:ls.words,source:"LEGACY-EMERGENCY"};
                    perf(report,"legacy-emergency",start,"≤1800ms");QF_CatalogReport.save(ctx,report);return legacy;
                }
            }catch(e){QF_CatalogReport.error(report,"legacy-emergency",e);}
            return [];
        }

        var tMerge=Date.now(),source=String(skeleton.source||"cleanroom"),merged=null,
            exactCpu=(source==="LEGACY-WEB-EXACT"||source==="LEGACY-WEB-SET-EXACT");
        if(exactCpu&&simple&&simple.model)merged=QF_CatalogMerger.mergeAligned(skeleton,simple.model);
        if(!merged)merged=QF_CatalogMerger.merge(skeleton,donors);
        phase.merge=Math.max(0,Date.now()-tMerge);
        report.merge={matched:merged.matched,timeFill:merged.timeFill,wordFill:merged.wordFill,titleMatch:merged.titleMatch,fast:String(merged.fast||"")};
        var mergedStats=merged.stats||QF_CatalogNormalizer.stats(merged.model);
        report.completeness={chapters:mergedStats.chapters,time:mergedStats.time,words:mergedStats.words,wordsOptional:true,wordDonor:"optional/unsupported"};

        var tAdapter=Date.now(),adapt=QF_LegadoCatalogAdapter.rowsWithStats(merged.model,{bookId:bid,bookName:meta.bookName,author:meta.author,source:source}),
            rows=adapt.rows,os=adapt.stats||QF_LegadoCatalogAdapter.stats(rows);
        phase.adapter=Math.max(0,Date.now()-tAdapter);
        report.output={chapters:os.chapters,time:os.time,words:os.words,source:source};
        perf(report,"full-refresh",start,"≤1500ms");
        report.performance.phases=phase;report.performance.over=Number(report.performance.elapsed||0)>1500;
        QF_CatalogReport.save(ctx,report);

        if(os.chapters>0){
            QF_CacheManager.set(ctx,key,{schema:CACHE_SCHEMA,rows:rows,stats:os,source:source,ts:now,lastChapterId:lastChapterIdFromRows(rows)});
            try{var ss=qfSource(ctx);if(ss&&ss.put)ss.put("qf_toc_last_v70","CLEAN41-R4 "+os.chapters+"章 / 时间"+os.time+"章 / 字数可选");}catch(_p){}
            return rows;
        }
        return [];
    }
    return {load:load,bookMeta:bookMeta,cacheKey:cacheKey};
})();

function qfCatalogLoad(raw,baseUrl){return QF_CatalogService.load.call(this,raw,baseUrl);}
function qfCatalogReportText(){return QF_CatalogReport.text(this);}
function qfCatalogReportShow(){
    var t=QF_CatalogReport.text(this),j=qfJava(this);try{if(j&&j.longToast){j.longToast(t);return;}}catch(_e){}try{if(j&&j.toast)j.toast(t);}catch(_e2){}
}


var QF_CFG40_MAP=null,QF_CFG40_TS=0;

function qfSettingsMapV40(ctx){
    var now=Date.now();
    if(QF_CFG40_MAP&&now-QF_CFG40_TS<500)return QF_CFG40_MAP;
    var m=null;
    try{var s=qfSource(ctx);m=s&&s.getLoginInfoMap?s.getLoginInfoMap():null;}catch(e){}
    QF_CFG40_MAP=m;QF_CFG40_TS=now;
    return m;
}
function qfSetting(key, def) {
    try {
        var m=qfSettingsMapV40(this);
        var v=m&&(m.get?m.get(key):m[key]);
        if(v!==null&&v!==undefined&&String(v).trim()!=="")return String(v);
    } catch(e) {}
    return def==null?"":String(def);
}
function qfToggle(key, def) {
    var v = qfSetting.call(this, key, def ? "✅" : "🔳").trim().toLowerCase();
    return !(v === "🔳" || v === "0" || v === "false" || v === "关" || v === "关闭");
}

/* =========================
 * 起点：页面列表 / 详情
 * ========================= */
function qdDoc(html) {
    try { return Packages.org.jsoup.Jsoup.parse(String(html || "")); }
    catch(e) { return null; }
}
function qdAbsQidian(href) {
    href = String(href || "").trim();
    if (!href) return "";
    if (/^\/\//.test(href)) return "https:" + href;
    if (/^\//.test(href)) return QF_QIDIAN + href;
    return href;
}
function qdPickText(root, selectors) {
    if (!root) return "";
    for (var i = 0; i < selectors.length; i++) {
        try {
            var e = root.select(selectors[i]).first();
            if (e) {
                var t = qfText(String(e.text()));
                if (t) return t;
            }
        } catch(err) {}
    }
    return "";
}
function qdPickAttr(root, selectors, attr) {
    if (!root) return "";
    for (var i = 0; i < selectors.length; i++) {
        try {
            var e = root.select(selectors[i]).first();
            if (e) {
                var t = String(e.attr(attr) || "");
                if (t) return qdAbsQidian(t);
            }
        } catch(err) {}
    }
    return "";
}
function qdBookIdFromUrl(url) {
    var m = String(url || "").match(/\/book\/(\d+)/i);
    return m ? m[1] : "";
}
function qdChapterMeta(url) {
    var raw = String(url || "");
    var m = raw.match(/\/chapter\/(\d+)\/(\d+)/i);
    var bid = m ? String(m[1]) : "";
    var cid = m ? String(m[2]) : "";
    function get(k) {
        var x = raw.match(new RegExp("[?&#]" + k + "=([^&#,]+)", "i"));
        if (!x) return "";
        try { return decodeURIComponent(x[1]); } catch(e) { return x[1]; }
    }
    var b = qfBook(this);
    var c = qfChapter(this);
    var name = get("qfBook");
    var author = get("qfAuthor");
    var title = get("qfTitle");
    var vip = get("qfVip");
    try {
        if (!name && b && b.getVariable) name = String(b.getVariable("qf_name") || "");
        if (!author && b && b.getVariable) author = String(b.getVariable("qf_author") || "");
        if (!bid && b && b.getVariable) bid = String(b.getVariable("qf_bid") || "");
    } catch(e1) {}
    try {
        if (!title && c) title = String(c.title || c.chapterName || "");
    } catch(e2) {}
    return {bookId:bid, chapterId:cid, bookName:name, author:author, title:title, vip:String(vip || "")};
}
function qdParseBookInfo(html, baseUrl) {
    var doc = qdDoc(html);
    var bid = qdBookIdFromUrl(baseUrl);
    var rawHtml=String(html||"");
    if (!bid && doc) {
        try {
            var c = doc.select('link[rel=canonical]').first();
            if (c) bid = qdBookIdFromUrl(String(c.attr("href") || ""));
        } catch(e0) {}
    }

    var name = qdPickText(doc, ['.book-info h1','h1','[class*=book-name]','[class*=bookName]']);
    var author = qdPickText(doc, ['.book-info a[href*="/author/"]','a[href*="/author/"]','.writer','[class*=author]']);
    /* beta7.4.1：当前书身份先锚定 URL / Book 对象。 */
    var expectedName=String(qfQueryV09(baseUrl,'qfSearchName')||'');
    var expectedAuthor=String(qfQueryV09(baseUrl,'qfSearchAuthor')||'');
    try{
        var _cb=qfBook(this);
        if(!expectedName&&_cb&&_cb.name)expectedName=String(_cb.name||'');
        if(!expectedAuthor&&_cb&&_cb.author)expectedAuthor=String(_cb.author||'');
    }catch(_idb){}
    expectedName=qfText(expectedName);expectedAuthor=qfText(expectedAuthor);
    if(expectedName)name=expectedName;
    if(expectedAuthor)author=expectedAuthor;
    /* beta7.4：只认“作品简介”本体，不再使用 meta description 兜底。
       起点 meta description 常是“作者创作的xx小说、已更新xx章……”SEO 摘要，
       与 APP / 起点助手展示的正式简介不是同一份文本。 */
    var intro = qdPickText(doc, [
        '.book-intro p',
        '.book-intro',
        '[class*=bookIntro] p',
        '[class*=bookIntro]',
        '[class*=book-intro] p',
        '[class*=book-intro]',
        '[id*=bookIntro]',
        '[id*=book-intro]',
        '.book-info .intro',
        '[class*=bookInfo] [class*=intro]',
        '[class*=book-info] [class*=intro]'
    ]);

    var cover = "";
    try {
        var og = doc && doc.select('meta[property=og:image]').first();
        if (og) cover = qdAbsQidian(og.attr("content"));
    } catch(e2) {}
    if (!cover) cover = qdPickAttr(doc, ['.book-img img','.book-cover img','img[src*=qdbimg]','img[data-src*=qdbimg]'], 'src');
    if (!cover) cover = qdPickAttr(doc, ['.book-img img','.book-cover img','img[data-src*=qdbimg]'], 'data-src');
    if(!cover&&bid)cover='https://bookcover.yuewen.com/qdbimg/349573/'+encodeURIComponent(bid)+'/300';
    if(/^\/\//.test(cover))cover='https:'+cover;

    var kind = qdPickText(doc, [
        '.book-info .tag a[href*="/all/"]',
        '.book-info p.tag a[href*="/all/"]',
        '.book-info .tag a',
        'p.tag a[href*="/all/"]'
    ]);
    (function(){
        var allow=['玄幻','奇幻','武侠','仙侠','都市','现实','军事','历史','游戏','体育','科幻','悬疑','轻小说','诸天无限','短篇'];
        function pick(s){
            s=String(s||'');
            for(var ai=0;ai<allow.length;ai++)if(s.indexOf(allow[ai])>=0)return allow[ai];
            return '';
        }
        var kk=pick(kind);
        if(!kk){
            var tagAll='';
            try{tagAll=qfText(String(doc&&doc.select?doc.select('.book-info .tag, p.tag, [class*=book-info] [class*=tag]').text():''));}catch(_ka){}
            kk=pick(tagAll);
        }
        /* 不在主分类白名单里的文本（例如作者名）不能继续冒充 kind。 */
        kind=kk||'';
    })();

    var last = "";
    try {
        var chs = doc && doc.select('a[href*="/chapter/"]');
        if (chs && chs.size()) last = qfText(chs.get(chs.size() - 1).text());
    } catch(e3) {}

    var pageText='',metricText='';
    try{pageText=qfText(String(doc&&doc.text?doc.text():''));}catch(_t){}
    try{
        var _core=doc&&doc.select?doc.select('.book-info, [class~=book-info], [class*=bookInfo], [class*=book-detail], [class*=bookDetail]').first():null;
        if(_core)metricText=qfText(String(_core.text()||''));
    }catch(_ct){}
    if(!metricText)metricText=pageText;

    function fmtDateTime(v){
        if(v===undefined||v===null||v==='')return '';
        var n=Number(v),d=null;
        if(isFinite(n)&&n>946684800){if(n<1e12)n*=1000;d=new Date(n);}
        else{
            var s=String(v).trim();
            if(!s)return '';
            var p=Date.parse(s.replace(/-/g,'/').replace(/年|月/g,'/').replace(/日/g,''));
            if(!isNaN(p))d=new Date(p);else return s;
        }
        function z(x){return x<10?'0'+x:String(x);}
        return d.getFullYear()+'-'+z(d.getMonth()+1)+'-'+z(d.getDate())+' '+z(d.getHours())+':'+z(d.getMinutes());
    }
    /* beta7.4.1：只解析嵌入数据里明确属于当前 bookId / 当前书名的对象。 */
    var currentJsonNodes=[];
    function ciVal(o,keys){
        return QF_Field.pickCI(o,keys,'',true);
    }
    function sameBookNode(o){
        if(!o||typeof o!=='object'||Array.isArray(o))return false;
        var id=String(ciVal(o,['bookId','BookId','bookID','BookID','bid'])||'');
        if(bid&&id&&id===String(bid))return true;
        var nn=qfText(ciVal(o,['bookName','BookName','name','Name','bookTitle','BookTitle'])||'');
        if(expectedName&&nn&&qfNorm(nn)===qfNorm(expectedName)){
            var aa=qfText(ciVal(o,['authorName','AuthorName','author','Author','writerName','WriterName'])||'');
            if(!expectedAuthor||!aa||qfNorm(aa)===qfNorm(expectedAuthor))return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    function walkCurrent(o,d,state){
        if(o==null||d>18||state.n>50000)return;state.n++;
        if(Array.isArray(o)){for(var i=0;i<o.length;i++)walkCurrent(o[i],d+1,state);return;}
        if(typeof o!=='object')return;
        if(sameBookNode(o))currentJsonNodes.push(o);
        for(var k in o)if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(o,k)&&o[k]&&typeof o[k]==='object')walkCurrent(o[k],d+1,state);
    }
    try{
        var _ss=doc&&doc.select?doc.select('script'):null;
        if(_ss)for(var _si=0;_si<_ss.size()&&currentJsonNodes.length<12;_si++){
            var _el=_ss.get(_si),_tx='';
            try{_tx=String(_el.data()||_el.html()||_el.text()||'');}catch(_sx){}
            if(!_tx)continue;
            if(bid&&_tx.indexOf(String(bid))<0&&(!expectedName||_tx.indexOf(expectedName)<0))continue;
            var _jr=null,_trim=_tx.trim();
            try{_jr=JSON.parse(_trim);}catch(_jp){
                try{var _a=_trim.indexOf('{'),_z=_trim.lastIndexOf('}');if(_a>=0&&_z>_a)_jr=JSON.parse(_trim.slice(_a,_z+1));}catch(_jp2){}
            }
            if(_jr)walkCurrent(_jr,0,{n:0});
        }
    }catch(_scripts){}
    function scopedField(node,keys,d){
        if(node==null||d>5)return '';
        if(Array.isArray(node)){for(var i=0;i<node.length;i++){var ar=scopedField(node[i],keys,d+1);if(ar!=='')return ar;}return '';}
        if(typeof node!=='object')return '';
        var v=ciVal(node,keys);if(v!=='')return v;
        for(var k in node)if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(node,k)&&node[k]&&typeof node[k]==='object'){
            var child=node[k],childId=String(ciVal(child,['bookId','BookId','bookID','BookID','bid'])||'');
            if(childId&&bid&&childId!==String(bid))continue;
            var r=scopedField(child,keys,d+1);if(r!=='')return r;
        }
        return '';
    }
    function rawField(keys){
        for(var i=0;i<currentJsonNodes.length;i++){var r=scopedField(currentJsonNodes[i],keys,0);if(r!=='')return String(r);}
        return '';
    }
    function rawLongField(keys){var r=rawField(keys);r=qfText(String(r||''));return r.length>=8?r:'';}
    if(!intro)intro=rawLongField(['bookIntro','BookIntro','bookIntroduction','BookIntroduction','intro','Intro']);

    /* 分类校正：主分类只允许起点一级大类；子分类单独保存。
       这样不会再把作者名误显示为“分类”，同时可还原“历史 · 秦汉三国”这类层级。 */
    var subKind='';
    (function(){
        var mains=['玄幻','奇幻','武侠','仙侠','都市','现实','军事','历史','游戏','体育','科幻','悬疑','轻小说','诸天无限','短篇'];
        function isMain(s){s=qfText(s);for(var i=0;i<mains.length;i++)if(s===mains[i])return true;return false;}
        function validSub(s){
            s=qfText(s).replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
            if(!s||s===author||s===name||isMain(s)||s.length>12)return '';
            if(/^(?:连载|连载中|完本|已完结|签约|VIP|免费|限免|起点中文网|男生|女生)$/.test(s))return '';
            return s;
        }
        try{
            var es=doc&&doc.select?doc.select('a[href*="/all/"], a[href*="chanId"], a[href*="subCateId"], .book-info .tag a, p.tag a'):null;
            if(es){
                var afterMain=false;
                for(var i=0;i<es.size();i++){
                    var t=qfText(es.get(i).text());
                    if(!t)continue;
                    if(isMain(t)){kind=t;afterMain=true;continue;}
                    if(afterMain&&!subKind){var s=validSub(t);if(s)subKind=s;}
                }
            }
        }catch(_ck){}
        var rk=rawField(['categoryName','CategoryName','channelName','ChannelName','bookCategoryName']);
        if(isMain(rk))kind=qfText(rk);
        var rs=rawField(['subCategoryName','SubCategoryName','subCateName','SubCateName','subCatName','SubCatName']);
        rs=validSub(rs);if(rs)subKind=rs;
        if(!isMain(kind))kind='';
    })();

    function reEsc(s){return String(s||'').replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&');}
    function safeAuthorLevel(){
        var r=rawField(['authorLevel','AuthorLevel','writerLevel','WriterLevel','authorLv','AuthorLv']);
        if(r){var n=String(r).match(/(\d{1,2})/);if(n)return 'Lv'+n[1];}
        var m=null;
        if(author){try{m=metricText.match(new RegExp(reEsc(author)+'[^\\n]{0,80}?(?:Lv|LV|等级)\\s*([0-9]{1,2})','i'));}catch(_al){}}
        return m&&m[1]?'Lv'+String(m[1]):'';
    }
    function safeAuthorWorksCount(){
        var r=rawField(['authorBookCount','AuthorBookCount','worksCount','WorksCount','authorWorksCount','AuthorWorksCount']);
        if(/^\d{1,3}$/.test(String(r||'')))return String(r);
        if(!author)return '';
        var m=null;
        try{m=metricText.match(new RegExp(reEsc(author)+'[^\\n]{0,120}?已有\\s*(\\d{1,3})\\s*本作品','i'));}catch(_aw){}
        return m&&m[1]?String(m[1]):'';
    }

  

===== QF_CatalogNormalizer pos=78060 =====
var QF_CatalogNormalizer=(function(){
    function arr(v){
        if(!v)return [];
        if(Array.isArray(v))return v;
        try{if(v.size&&v.get){var a=[];for(var i=0;i<v.size();i++)a.push(v.get(i));return a;}}catch(_e){}
        return [];
    }
    function dataRoot(root){
        root=root||{};
        if(root.Data!==undefined&&root.Data!==null)return root.Data;
        if(root.data!==undefined&&root.data!==null)return root.data;
        return root;
    }
    function pick(o,keys,def){return QF_Field.pick(o,keys,def);}
    function text(v){return String(v==null?"":v).replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();}
    function truthy(v){if(v===true||v===1)return true;return /^(?:1|true|yes)$/i.test(String(v==null?"":v));}
    function chapter(o,defaults){
        o=o||{};defaults=defaults||{};
        var id=text(pick(o,QF_CatalogSchema.chapterId,""));
        var name=text(pick(o,QF_CatalogSchema.chapterName,""));
        if(!id||!name)return null;
        var explicit=pick(o,QF_CatalogSchema.vip,null),vip=false;
        if(explicit!==null&&explicit!==undefined&&String(explicit)!=="")vip=truthy(explicit);
        else{
            var ss=pick(o,QF_CatalogSchema.subscribeStatus,null);
            if(ss!==null&&ss!==undefined&&String(ss)!=="")vip=Number(ss)===0;
            else vip=!!defaults.vip;
        }
        return {
            id:id,name:name,vip:vip,
            time:pick(o,QF_CatalogSchema.updateTime,""),
            words:pick(o,QF_CatalogSchema.wordCount,""),
            volumeId:text(pick(o,QF_CatalogSchema.volumeId,defaults.volumeId||"")),
            volumeName:text(pick(o,QF_CatalogSchema.volumeName,defaults.volumeName||"")),
            source:String(defaults.source||""),index:-1
        };
    }
    function fromVolumes(root,source){
        var d=dataRoot(root),vols=arr(d&&(d.vs||d.Vs||d.Volumes||d.volumes||d.VolumeList||d.volumeList)),out=[],seen={},volInfo=[];
        if(!vols.length){
            var direct=arr(d&&(d.cs||d.Cs||d.Chapters||d.chapters||d.chapterList||d.ChapterList));
            if(direct.length)vols=[{vN:"正文",cs:direct}];
        }
        for(var vi=0;vi<vols.length;vi++){
            var vo=vols[vi]||{},vn=text(pick(vo,QF_CatalogSchema.volumeName,"")),vid=text(pick(vo,QF_CatalogSchema.volumeId,""));
            var cs=arr(vo.cs||vo.Cs||vo.Chapters||vo.chapters||vo.chapterList||vo.ChapterList||vo.VolumeList||vo.volumeList),start=out.length;
            for(var ci=0;ci<cs.length;ci++){
                var c=chapter(cs[ci],{volumeId:vid,volumeName:vn,source:source});if(!c||seen[c.id])continue;seen[c.id]=1;c.index=out.length;out.push(c);
            }
            if(out.length>start)volInfo.push({id:vid,name:vn,start:start,end:out.length-1,count:out.length-start,words:pick(vo,["wC","WC","wordCount","WordCount","wordsCount","WordsCount"],"")});
        }
        return {chapters:out,volumes:volInfo,source:String(source||""),shape:"volumes"};
    }
    function scan(root,source,maxNodes){
        var out=[],seen={},state={n:0,max:Number(maxNodes||40000)};
        function walk(n,depth,vol){
            if(n===undefined||n===null||depth>12||state.n>state.max)return;
            if(Array.isArray(n)){for(var ai=0;ai<n.length;ai++)walk(n[ai],depth+1,vol);return;}
            if(typeof n!=="object")return;state.n++;
            var vn=text(pick(n,QF_CatalogSchema.volumeName,vol&&vol.name||"")),vid=text(pick(n,QF_CatalogSchema.volumeId,vol&&vol.id||""));
            var c=chapter(n,{volumeId:vid,volumeName:vn,source:source});
            if(c&&!seen[c.id]){seen[c.id]=1;c.index=out.length;out.push(c);}
            for(var k in n)if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(n,k)){
                var v=n[k];if(v&&typeof v==="object")walk(v,depth+1,{id:vid,name:vn});
            }
        }
        walk(dataRoot(root),0,null);
        return {chapters:out,volumes:[],source:String(source||""),shape:"scan",nodes:state.n};
    }
    function fromHtml(html,source){
        var out=[],volumes=[],seen={},current="";
        if(!html)return {chapters:out,volumes:volumes,source:String(source||""),shape:"html"};
        try{
            var doc=Packages.org.jsoup.Jsoup.parse(String(html||""));
            var es=doc.select("div[class*=chapterBar],a[class*=chapterItem]");
            for(var i=0;i<es.size();i++){
                var el=es.get(i),cls=String(el.className()||""),txt=text(el.text());
                if(cls.indexOf("chapterBar")>=0){current=txt;continue;}
                if(cls.indexOf("chapterItem")<0)continue;
                var h=el.select("h2").first();if(!h)h=el.select("h3").first();var title=h?text(h.text()):txt;
                title=title.replace(/\s*(?:App\s*免费|APP\s*免费)\s*$/i,"").trim();
                var href=String(el.attr("href")||""),m=href.match(/\/chapter\/(\d+)\/(\d+)/i),cid=m&&m[2]?String(m[2]):"";
                if(!cid||!title||seen[cid])continue;seen[cid]=1;
                var vip=cls.indexOf("unPay")>=0||/(?:App\s*免费|APP\s*免费|订阅|VIP)/i.test(txt),tm="",wc="";
                try{var te=el.select("time").first();if(te)tm=String(te.attr("datetime")||te.text()||"").trim();}catch(_t){}
                if(!tm){var dm=txt.match(/20\d{2}[-\/.年]\d{1,2}[-\/.月]\d{1,2}(?:日)?(?:\s+\d{1,2}:\d{2})?/);if(dm)tm=dm[0];}
                var wm=txt.match(/(?:字数[：:]?\s*)?([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?(?:万|千|百)?\s*字)/);if(wm)wc=wm[1];
                out.push({id:cid,name:title,vip:vip,time:tm,words:wc,volumeId:"",volumeName:current,source:String(source||"html"),index:out.length});
            }
        }catch(e){try{QF_Diagnostic.warn(null,"catalog","normalizer","html",e);}catch(_e){}}
        return {chapters:out,volumes:volumes,source:String(source||""),shape:"html"};
    }
    function fromLegacyRows(rows,source){
        var out=[],seen={},volumes=[],currentVolume="",a=Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[];
        function timeFromInfo(v){
            var s=String(v==null?"":v).trim();if(!s)return "";
            var m=s.match(/(20\d{2}[-\/.年]\d{1,2}[-\/.月]\d{1,2}(?:日)?(?:\s+\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?)?)/);if(m)return m[1];
            var n=s.match(/\b(1\d{9}|1\d{12})\b/);return n?n[1]:"";
        }
        function wordsFromInfo(v){
            var s=String(v==null?"":v).trim(),m=s.match(/([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?(?:万|千|百)?\s*字)/);return m?m[1]:"";
        }
        for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){
            var x=a[i]||{},name=text(x.N!==undefined?x.N:(x.name!==undefined?x.name:"")),url=String(x.C!==undefined?x.C:(x.url!==undefined?x.url:""));
            if(x.Vo===true||x.volume===true||(!url&&name&&name!=="版权信息")){currentVolume=name;continue;}
            if(!name||name==="版权信息"||!url)continue;
            var m=url.match(/\/chapter\/\d+\/(\d+)/i)||url.match(/[?&#](?:chapterId|cid)=(\d+)/i),cid=m&&m[1]?String(m[1]):"";
            if(!cid||seen[cid])continue;seen[cid]=1;
            var info=String(x.T!==undefined?x.T:(x.info!==undefined?x.info:""));
            out.push({id:cid,name:name,vip:!!x.V,time:timeFromInfo(info),words:wordsFromInfo(info),volumeId:"",volumeName:currentVolume,source:String(source||"LEGACY-SKELETON"),index:out.length});
        }
        return {chapters:out,volumes:volumes,source:String(source||"LEGACY-SKELETON"),shape:"legacyRows"};
    }
    function stats(model){
        var c=model&&Array.isArray(model.chapters)?model.chapters:[],z={chapters:0,time:0,words:0};
        for(var i=0;i<c.length;i++){
            var x=c[i]||{};if(!x.id||!x.name)continue;z.chapters++;
            if(x.time!==undefined&&x.time!==null&&String(x.time).trim()!=="")z.time++;
            if(x.words!==undefined&&x.words!==null&&String(x.words).trim()!=="")z.words++;
        }
        return z;
    }
        function simpleList(root,source){
        var d=dataRoot(root),list=null;
        if(Array.isArray(d))list=d;
        else if(d&&typeof d==="object"){
            var x=d.list||d.List||d.items||d.Items||d.chapterList||d.ChapterList;
            if(Array.isArray(x))list=x;
        }
        if(!Array.isArray(list)||!list.length)return null;
        var out=[],seen={},volumes=[],lastVol="",volStart=-1;
        for(var i=0;i<list.length;i++){
            var o=list[i]||{},
                id=text(o.chapterId!==undefined?o.chapterId:(o.ChapterId!==undefined?o.ChapterId:(o.id!==undefined?o.id:o.Id))),
                name=text(o.chapterName!==undefined?o.chapterName:(o.ChapterName!==undefined?o.ChapterName:(o.name!==undefined?o.name:o.Name)));
            if(!id||!name||seen[id])continue;
            seen[id]=1;
            var vn=text(o.volumeName!==undefined?o.volumeName:(o.VolumeName!==undefined?o.VolumeName:(o.vN!==undefined?o.vN:o.VN))),
                vid=text(o.volumeId!==undefined?o.volumeId:(o.VolumeId!==undefined?o.VolumeId:(o.vId!==undefined?o.vId:o.VId))),
                tm=o.time!==undefined?o.time:(o.Time!==undefined?o.Time:(o.uT!==undefined?o.uT:o.UT)),
                wc=o.wordCount!==undefined?o.wordCount:(o.WordCount!==undefined?o.WordCount:"");
            if(vn!==lastVol){
                if(lastVol&&volStart>=0)volumes.push({id:"",name:lastVol,start:volStart,end:out.length-1,count:out.length-volStart,words:""});
                lastVol=vn;volStart=out.length;
            }
            out.push({id:id,name:name,vip:false,time:tm,words:wc,volumeId:vid,volumeName:vn,source:String(source||""),index:out.length});
        }
        if(lastVol&&volStart>=0)volumes.push({id:"",name:lastVol,start:volStart,end:out.length-1,count:out.length-volStart,words:""});
        if(!out.length)return null;
        return {chapters:out,volumes:volumes,source:String(source||""),shape:"simple-list-fast",nodes:out.length,fastSimple:true};
    }
return {arr:arr,dataRoot:dataRoot,chapter:chapter,fromVolumes:fromVolumes,scan:scan,simpleList:simpleList,fromHtml:fromHtml,fromLegacyRows:fromLegacyRows,stats:stats};
})();

var QF_CatalogClient=(function(){
    function app(ctx,path,params,tag,timeout){
        var st=Date.now();
        try{
            var r=qfOfficialCallV400.call(ctx,"appRequest",[path,params||{},{bookId:String(params&&params.bookId||""),timeout:Number(timeout||9000),force:true,noCache:true}]);
            var root=r&&r.json?r.json:{},raw=String(r&&r.raw||""),ok=!!(r&&r.ok&&root&&typeof root==="object");
            var detail="code="+String(r&&r.code||"")+(r&&r.message?" msg="+String(r.message):"");
            if(!root||Object.keys(root).length===0)detail+=(raw?" raw="+raw.slice(0,100).replace(/[\r\n\t]+/g," "):" empty-json");
            return {ok:ok,root:root,raw:raw,code:String(r&&r.code||""),message:String(r&&r.message||""),tag:String(tag||path),ms:Date.now()-st,detail:detail};
        }catch(e){return {ok:false,root:{},raw:"",code:"EXCEPTION",message:String(e&&e.message||e),tag:String(tag||path),ms:Date.now()-st,detail:String(e&&e.message||e)};}
    }
    function v3(ctx,bid){
        var tries=[
            {tag:"APP-v3",params:{bookId:bid,timeStamp:0,requestSource:0,md5Signature:"",extendchapterIds:""}},
            {tag:"APP-v3-split",params:{bookId:bid,timeStamp:0,requestSource:0,md5Signature:"",extendchapterIds:"",splitEpub:1}}
        ],best=null;
        for(var i=0;i<tries.length;i++){
            var r=app(ctx,"/argus/api/v3/chapterlist/chapterlist",tries[i].params,tries[i].tag,9500),m=QF_CatalogNormalizer.fromVolumes(r.root,tries[i].tag),s=QF_CatalogNormalizer.stats(m);
            r.model=m;r.stats=s;
            if(!best||s.chapters>best.stats.chapters||(s.chapters===best.stats.chapters&&s.time>best.stats.time))best=r;
            if(s.chapters>0&&s.time>=Math.max(3,Math.floor(s.chapters*0.60)))break;
        }
        return best||{ok:false,root:{},model:{chapters:[],volumes:[]},stats:{chapters:0,time:0,words:0},tag:"APP-v3",ms:0,detail:"no-response"};
    }
    function simple(ctx,bid,needCount){
        var known=Number(needCount||0)>0,modes=known?[0,1]:[1],best=null,need=known?Math.max(3,Math.floor(Number(needCount||0)*0.60)):0,attempts=[];
        /* alpha10.6.7：
         * 冷启动只取 showrelate=1。最终章节集合由骨架控制，simple 只按 ChapterId 填时间，
         * 因此无需为 mode0 再付一次网络 + 全量 JSON scan 成本。
         * warm probe 仍保持 mode0 优先，避免改变缓存增量判定。 */
        for(var i=0;i<modes.length;i++){
            var tag="APP-simple"+modes[i],r=app(ctx,"/argus/api/v2/chapterlist/getsimple",{bookId:bid,showrelate:modes[i]},tag,9500),m=QF_CatalogNormalizer.simpleList(r.root,tag)||QF_CatalogNormalizer.scan(r.root,tag,50000),s=QF_CatalogNormalizer.stats(m);
            r.model=m;r.stats=s;
            attempts.push({tag:tag,ms:Number(r.ms||0),chapters:Number(s.chapters||0),time:Number(s.time||0),code:String(r.code||"")});
            if(!best){best=r;}
            else if(known){if(s.time>best.stats.time||(s.time===best.stats.time&&s.chapters>best.stats.chapters))best=r;}
            else{if(s.chapters>best.stats.chapters||(s.chapters===best.stats.chapters&&s.time>best.stats.time))best=r;}
            if(known&&s.time>=need&&need>0)break;
        }
        if(best){
            best.attempts=attempts;
            var ad=[];for(var ai=0;ai<attempts.length;ai++){var a=attempts[ai];ad.push(a.tag+"="+a.chapters+"章/"+a.time+"时/"+a.ms+"ms"+(a.code?("/"+a.code):""));}
            best.detail=(String(best.detail||"")+(ad.length?(" | attempts "+ad.join(" ; ")):"")).trim();
        }
        return best||{ok:false,root:{},model:{chapters:[],volumes:[]},stats:{chapters:0,time:0,words:0},tag:"APP-simple",ms:0,detail:"no-response",attempts:attempts};
    }
    function pager(ctx,bid,baseModel,opt){
        opt=opt||{};var cs=baseModel&&Array.isArray(baseModel.chapters)?baseModel.chapters:[],ids=[],seen={},tag=String(opt.tag||"APP-pager"),all={chapters:[],volumes:[],source:tag},totalMs=0,errors=[];
        if(!cs.length)return {ok:false,model:all,stats:{chapters:0,time:0,words:0},tag:tag,ms:0,detail:"no-anchor"};
        var anchorCount=Math.max(3,Math.min(11,Number(opt.anchors||7)));if(cs.length<anchorCount)anchorCount=cs.length;
        for(var p=0;p<anchorCount;p++){
            var at=anchorCount<=1?0:Math.round((cs.length-1)*(p/(anchorCount-1))),cid=String(cs[at]&&cs[at].id||"");if(!cid||seen[cid])continue;seen[cid]=1;ids.push(cid);
        }
        var map={};
        for(var i=0;i<ids.length;i++){
            var r=app(ctx,"/argus/api/v1/chapterlist/getchapterlistbypager",{bookId:bid,chapterId:ids[i]},tag,8500);totalMs+=Number(r.ms||0);
            if(!r.ok)errors.push(r.code||r.message||"pager-failed");
            var mm=QF_CatalogNormalizer.scan(r.root,tag,20000),mc=mm.chapters||[];
            for(var j=0;j<mc.length;j++){var x=mc[j];if(x&&x.id&&!map[x.id])map[x.id]=x;}
        }
        for(var k in map)if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(map,k)){var xx=map[k];xx.index=all.chapters.length;all.chapters.push(xx);}
        return {ok:all.chapters.length>0,model:all,stats:QF_CatalogNormalizer.stats(all),tag:tag,ms:totalMs,detail:"anchors="+ids.length+(errors.length?" errors="+errors.length:"")};
    }
    function webCategory(ctx,bid){
        var st=Date.now(),tk="",ck="";
        try{tk=String(qdCsrf.call(ctx)||"");}catch(_tk){}
        try{ck=String(cookie.getCookie("https://www.qidian.com")||cookie.getCookie("https://read.qidian.com")||cookie.getCookie("https://vipreader.qidian.com")||cookie.getCookie("https://m.qidian.com")||"");}catch(_ck){}

        if(tk){urls.push("https://vipreader.qidian.com/ajax/book/category?bookId="+encodeURIComponent(bid));urls.push("https://read.qidian.com/ajax/book/category?bookId="+encodeURIComponent(bid));}
        var headers={"User-Agent":QF_UA,"Accept":"application/json,text/plain,*/*","Referer":"https://www.qidian.com/book/"+encodeURIComponent(bid)+"/","Cache-Control":"no-cache"};if(ck)headers.Cookie=ck;
        var best=null;
        for(var i=0;i<urls.length;i++){

            try{r0=qfAjaxTextV20(ctx,urls[i],{headers:headers,timeout:9000})||"";root=qfJson(r0,{})||{};}catch(e){root={};}
            var m=QF_CatalogNormalizer.fromVolumes(root,i%2===0?"WEB-vipreader":"WEB-read"),s=QF_CatalogNormalizer.stats(m),r={ok:s.chapters>0,root:root,raw:String(r0||""),model:m,stats:s,tag:m.source,ms:Date.now()-st,detail:s.chapters?"":"empty"};
            if(!best||s.time>best.stats.time||(s.time===best.stats.time&&s.chapters>best.stats.chapters))best=r;
            if(s.chapters>0&&s.time>=Math.max(3,Math.floor(s.chapters*0.60)))break;
        }
        return best||{ok:false,model:{chapters:[],volumes:[]},stats:{chapters:0,time:0,words:0},tag:"WEB-category",ms:Date.now()-st,detail:"empty"};
    }
        function simpleFromRaw(ctx,bid,raw,tag,ms){
        raw=String(raw||"");tag=String(tag||"APP-simple1");
        var root=QF_OfficialClient.parseJson(raw),code="",message="",ok=false;
        try{
            code=String(root&&(root.code!==undefined?root.code:(root.Code!==undefined?root.Code:""))||"");
            message=String(root&&(root.message||root.Message||root.msg||root.Msg)||"");
            ok=!!raw&&root&&typeof root==="object"&&Object.keys(root).length>0;
            if(code&&code!=="0"&&code!=="1"&&code!=="200"&&!(root.data!==undefined||root.Data!==undefined))ok=false;
        }catch(_p){ok=false;}
        var m=QF_CatalogNormalizer.simpleList(root,tag)||QF_CatalogNormalizer.scan(root,tag,50000),s=QF_CatalogNormalizer.stats(m),
            r={ok:ok,root:root,raw:raw,code:code,message:message,tag:tag,ms:Number(ms||0),model:m,stats:s,attempts:[]};
        r.attempts=[{tag:tag,ms:Number(ms||0),chapters:Number(s.chapters||0),time:Number(s.time||0),code:code}];
        r.detail="parallel-shared "+(m&&m.fastSimple?"simple-list-fast":"generic-scan")+" | attempts "+tag+"="+s.chapters+"章/"+s.time+"时/"+Number(ms||0)+"ms"+(code?("/"+code):"");
        return r;
    }
    function parallelCold(ctx,bid){
        bid=String(bid||"");var j=qfAjaxJavaV20(ctx);
        if(!bid||!j||typeof j.ajaxAll!=="function")return {ok:false,reason:"ajaxAll-unavailable",simple:null,webRaw:"",ms:0};
        var p0={bookId:bid,showrelate:0},p1={bookId:bid,showrelate:1},s0=null,s1=null;
        try{s0=QF_OfficialClient.appSpec(ctx,"v2/chapterlist/getsimple",p0,{timeout:9500});}catch(_s0){s0=null;}
        try{s1=QF_OfficialClient.appSpec(ctx,"v2/chapterlist/getsimple",p1,{timeout:9500});}catch(_s1){s1=null;}
        if(!s0||!s0.url||!s0.headers||!s1||!s1.url||!s1.headers)return {ok:false,reason:"simple-spec-failed",simple:null,webRaw:"",ms:0};
        var webUrl="https://m.qidian.com/book/"+encodeURIComponent(bid)+"/catalog/",
            o0={method:"GET",timeout:9500,headers:s0.headers},
            o1={method:"GET",timeout:9500,headers:s1.headers},
            wo={timeout:10000,headers:{"User-Agent":QF_UA,"Accept":"text/html,application/xhtml+xml","Referer":"https://m.qidian.com/book/"+encodeURIComponent(bid)+"/","Cache-Control":"no-cache"}},
            specs=[s0.url+","+JSON.stringify(o0),s1.url+","+JSON.stringify(o1),webUrl+","+JSON.stringify(wo)],
            st=Date.now(),arr=null;
        try{arr=j.ajaxAll(specs);}catch(_all){arr=null;}
        var elapsed=Math.max(0,Date.now()-st),r0="",r1="",webRaw="";
        if(arr&&arr.length>=3){
            try{r0=String(arr[0]&&typeof arr[0].body==="function"?arr[0].body():"");}catch(_r0){}
            try{r1=String(arr[1]&&typeof arr[1].body==="function"?arr[1].body():"");}catch(_r1){}
            try{webRaw=String(arr[2]&&typeof arr[2].body==="function"?arr[2].body():"");}catch(_rw){}
        }
        var m0=r0?simpleFromRaw(ctx,bid,r0,"APP-simple0",elapsed):null,
            m1=r1?simpleFromRaw(ctx,bid,r1,"APP-simple1",elapsed):null;
        try{
            if(r0){
                var e0={ok:!!(m0&&m0.ok),raw:r0,json:m0?m0.root:{},code:String(m0&&m0.code||""),message:String(m0&&m0.message||"")};
                QF_OfficialClient.observeExternal(ctx,"app","/argus/api/v2/chapterlist/getsimple",p0,e0,elapsed,{noCache:true});
            }
            if(r1){
                var e1={ok:!!(m1&&m1.ok),raw:r1,json:m1?m1.root:{},code:String(m1&&m1.code||""),message:String(m1&&m1.message||"")};
                QF_OfficialClient.observeExternal(ctx,"app","/argus/api/v2/chapterlist/getsimple",p1,e1,elapsed,{noCache:true});
            }
        }catch(_obs){}
        function webUniqueCount(raw){
            raw=String(raw||"");if(!raw)return 0;
            /* Qidian mobile catalog may embed chapter URLs inside JSON:
               \/chapter\/... or \u002Fchapter\u002F... .
               This preselector only counts IDs; final correctness still belongs to WEB-EXACT/SET-EXACT. */
            var norm=raw
                .replace(/\\u002[fF]/g,"/")
                .replace(/\\\//g,"/")
                .replace(/&#x2[fF];/gi,"/")
                .replace(/&#47;/gi,"/"),
                safeBid=String(bid||"").replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,"\\$&"),
                re=new RegExp("/chapter/"+safeBid+"/(\\d+)","ig"),
                seen={},n=0,m;
            while((m=re.exec(norm))){
                var id=String(m[1]||"");
                if(id&&!seen[id]){seen[id]=1;n++;}
                if(n>60000)break;
            }
            return n;
        }
        function valid(x){return !!(x&&x.stats&&Number(x.stats.chapters||0)>0);}
        function better(a,b){
            if(!valid(a))return b;if(!valid(b))return a;
            var ac=Number(a.stats.chapters||0),bc=Number(b.stats.chapters||0),
                at=Number(a.stats.time||0),bt=Number(b.stats.time||0);
            if(at!==bt)return at>bt?a:b;
            if(ac!==bc)return ac>bc?a:b;
            return String(b.tag||"").indexOf("simple1")>=0?b:a;
        }
        var wc=webUniqueCount(webRaw),pick=null,reason="";
        if(wc>0){
            var e0c=valid(m0)&&Number(m0.stats.chapters||0)===wc,
                e1c=valid(m1)&&Number(m1.stats.chapters||0)===wc;
            if(e0c&&e1c){pick=better(m0,m1);reason="web-count-both";}
            else if(e0c){pick=m0;reason="web-count-simple0";}
            else if(e1c){pick=m1;reason="web-count-simple1";}
        }
        if(!pick){pick=better(m0,m1);reason=reason||"coverage-fallback";}
        if(pick){
            var all=[];
            if(valid(m0))all.push("s0="+m0.stats.chapters+"/"+m0.stats.time);
            if(valid(m1))all.push("s1="+m1.stats.chapters+"/"+m1.stats.time);
            pick.detail="dual-parallel "+reason+" webUnique="+wc+" extractor="+(wc>0?"escaped-url-ok":"no-id")+" "+all.join(" ")+" | "+String(pick.detail||"");
        }
        return {ok:valid(pick),reason:reason||"simple-empty",simple:pick,simple0:m0,simple1:m1,webRaw:webRaw,webUnique:wc,ms:elapsed,parallel:true};
    }
return {app:app,v3:v3,simple:simple,pager:pager,webCategory:webCategory,simpleFromRaw:simpleFromRaw,parallelCold:parallelCold};
})();

var QF_CatalogMerger=(function(){
    function normTitle(v){
        try{return qfNormChapter(v);}catch(_e){return String(v||"").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,"");}
    }
    function merge(base,donors){
        base=base&&Array.isArray(base.chapters)?base:{chapters:[],volumes:[],source:""};donors=Array.isArray(donors)?donors:[];
        var idMap={},titleMap={},matched=0,timeFill=0,wordFill=0,titleMatch=0;
        for(var di=0;di<donors.length;di++){
            var d=donors[di]&&Array.isArray(donors[di].chapters)?donors[di].chapters:[];
            for(var i=0;i<d.length;i++){
                var x=d[i]||{};if(!x.id)continue;
                var old=idMap[x.id];
                if(!old||(!String(old.time||"")&&String(x.time||""))||(!String(old.words||"")&&String(x.words||"")))idMap[x.id]=x;
                var nt=normTitle(x.name);if(nt&&!titleMap[nt])titleMap[nt]=x;
            }
        }
        var c=base.chapters;
        for(var j=0;j<c.length;j++){
            var b=c[j]||{},m=idMap[b.id],byTitle=false;
            if(!m){var ntb=normTitle(b.name);if(ntb&&titleMap[ntb]){m=titleMap[ntb];byTitle=true;}}
            if(!m)continue;matched++;if(byTitle)titleMatch++;
            if((b.time===undefined||b.time===null||String(b.time).trim()==="")&&m.time!==undefined&&m.time!==null&&String(m.time).trim()!==""){b.time=m.time;timeFill++;}
            if((b.words===undefined||b.words===null||String(b.words).trim()==="")&&m.words!==undefined&&m.words!==null&&String(m.words).trim()!==""){b.words=m.words;wordFill++;}
            if(!b.volumeName&&m.volumeName)b.volumeName=m.volumeName;if(!b.volumeId&&m.volumeId)b.volumeId=m.volumeId;
        }
        for(var k=0;k<c.length;k++)c[k].index=k;
        return {model:base,matched:matched,timeFill:timeFill,wordFill:wordFill,titleMatch:titleMatch};
    }
        function mergeAligned(base,donor){
        if(!base||!Array.isArray(base.chapters)||!donor||!Array.isArray(donor.chapters))return null;
        var c=base.chapters,d=donor.chapters;if(!c.length||c.length!==d.length)return null;
        var matched=0,timeFill=0,wordFill=0,chapters=0,time=0,words=0;
        for(var i=0;i<c.length;i++){
            var b=c[i]||{},x=d[i]||{};
            if(!b.id||!x.id||String(b.id)!==String(x.id))return null;
            if(!b.name)return null;
            matched++;
            if((b.time===undefined||b.time===null||String(b.time).trim()==="")&&x.time!==undefined&&x.time!==null&&String(x.time).trim()!==""){b.time=x.time;timeFill++;}
            if((b.words===undefined||b.words===null||String(b.words).trim()==="")&&x.words!==undefined&&x.words!==null&&String(x.words).trim()!==""){b.words=x.words;wordFill++;}
            if(!b.volumeName&&x.volumeName)b.volumeName=x.volumeName;
            if(!b.volumeId&&x.volumeId)b.volumeId=x.volumeId;
            b.index=i;chapters++;
            if(b.time!==undefined&&b.time!==null&&String(b.time).trim()!=="")time++;
            if(b.words!==undefined&&b.words!==null&&String(b.words).trim()!=="")words++;
        }
        return {model:base,matched:matched,timeFill:timeFill,wordFill:wordFill,titleMatch:0,stats:{chapters:chapters,time:time,words:words},fast:"ALIGNED-ID"};
    }
return {merge:merge,mergeAligned:mergeAligned};
})();

var QF_LegadoCatalogAdapter=(function(){
    function fmtTime(v){
        if(v===undefined||v===null||v==="")return "";
        var raw=String(v).trim(),n=Number(raw),d=null;
        if(isFinite(n)&&n>946684800){if(n<1e12)n*=1000;d=new Date(n);}
        if(!d){
            var em=raw.match(/(?:^|\D)(1\d{9}|1\d{12})(?:\D|$)/);if(em){var en=Number(em[1]);if(en<1e12)en*=1000;d=new Date(en);}
        }
        if(!d){
            var clean=raw.replace(/年/g,"-").replace(/月/g,"-").replace(/日/g," ").replace(/[T]/g," ").replace(/\.(?=\d{1,2}(?:\D|$))/g,"-").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();
            var p=Date.parse(clean.replace(/-/g,"/"));if(!isNaN(p))d=new Date(p);
        }
        if(!d||isNaN(d.getTime()))return raw;
        function z(x){return x<10?"0"+x:String(x);}return d.getFullYear()+"-"+z(d.getMonth()+1)+"-"+z(d.getDate())+" "+z(d.getHours())+":"+z(d.getMinutes());
    }
    function fmtWords(v){
        if(v===undefined||v===null||v==="")return "";var s=String(v).replace(/,/g,"").trim(),m=s.match(/([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)(万|千|百)?\s*字?/i);if(!m)return "";var n=Number(m[1]);if(!isFinite(n)||n<=0)return "";return (Math.floor(n)===n?String(Math.floor(n)):String(n))+String(m[2]||"")+"字";
    }
    function info(tm,wc){var t=fmtTime(tm),w=fmtWords(wc);return t&&w?t+"  "+w:(t||w||"");}
    function chapterUrl(bookId,c,bookName,author,src){
        var u="https://www.qidian.com/chapter/"+encodeURIComponent(String(bookId||""))+"/"+encodeURIComponent(String(c.id||""))+"/";
        u+="?qfVip="+(c.vip?"1":"0")+"&qfTitle="+encodeURIComponent(String(c.name||""))+"&qfBook="+encodeURIComponent(String(bookName||""))+"&qfAuthor="+encodeURIComponent(String(author||""))+"&qfTocSrc="+encodeURIComponent(String(src||"cleanroom"))+"&qfIndex="+Math.max(0,Number(c.index||0));
        if(/地图|插图|彩蛋|设定图|人物图|示意图|平面图|世界图|区域图/i.test(String(c.name||"")))u+="&qfMediaRev=67";return u;
    }
    function copyrightRow(bookId,bookName,author){
        return {N:"版权信息",C:"https://m.qidian.com/book/"+encodeURIComponent(String(bookId||""))+"/?qfType=copyright&qfBook="+encodeURIComponent(String(bookName||""))+"&qfAuthor="+encodeURIComponent(String(author||""))+"&qfCv=3101",V:false,Vo:false,T:""};
    }
    function rows(model,meta){
        model=model||{chapters:[],volumes:[]};meta=meta||{};var out=[copyrightRow(meta.bookId,meta.bookName,meta.author)],cs=Array.isArray(model.chapters)?model.chapters:[];
        var lastVol=null;
        for(var i=0;i<cs.length;i++){
            var c=cs[i]||{};if(!c.id||!c.name)continue;
            var vn=String(c.volumeName||"").trim();
            if(vn&&vn!==lastVol){out.push({N:vn,C:"",V:false,Vo:true,T:""});lastVol=vn;}
            c.index=i;out.push({N:String(c.name),C:chapterUrl(meta.bookId,c,meta.bookName,meta.author,meta.source||model.source),V:!!c.vip,Vo:false,T:info(c.time,c.words)});
        }
        return out;
    }
    function stats(rows){var z={chapters:0,time:0,words:0},a=Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[];for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){var x=a[i]||{};if(x.Vo||x.N==="版权信息"||!x.C)continue;z.chapters++;var t=String(x.T||"");if(/20\d{2}[-\/.年]/.test(t)||/\d{10,13}/.test(t))z.time++;if(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:万|千|百)?字/.test(t))z.words++;}return z;}
        function rowsWithStats(model,meta){
        model=model||{chapters:[],volumes:[]};meta=meta||{};
        var out=[copyrightRow(meta.bookId,meta.bookName,meta.author)],cs=Array.isArray(model.chapters)?model.chapters:[],
            lastVol=null,z={chapters:0,time:0,words:0};
        for(var i=0;i<cs.length;i++){
            var c=cs[i]||{};if(!c.id||!c.name)continue;
            var vn=String(c.volumeName||"").trim();
            if(vn&&vn!==lastVol){out.push({N:vn,C:"",V:false,Vo:true,T:""});lastVol=vn;}
            c.index=i;
            var inf=info(c.time,c.words);
            out.push({N:String(c.name),C:chapterUrl(meta.bookId,c,meta.bookName,meta.author,meta.source||model.source),V:!!c.vip,Vo:false,T:inf});
            z.chapters++;
            if(/20\d{2}[-\/.年]/.test(inf)||/\d{10,13}/.test(inf))z.time++;
            if(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:万|千|百)?字/.test(inf))z.words++;
        }
        try{out._qfStats=z;}catch(_s){}
        return {rows:out,stats:z};
    }
return {rows:rows,rowsWithStats:rowsWithStats,stats:stats,fmtTime:fmtTime,fmtWords:fmtWords,info:info,chapterUrl:chapterUrl};
})();
